We introduce a functional score framework that extends the traditional estimating equations for survival data to nonparametric HTE and generalize the Neyman orthogonality accordingly, thus filling a methodological as well as theoretical gap. Under the Neyman orthogonal functional score framework, we developed the martingale R-learner based on a decomposition of the conditional martingale residuals into residuals of the risk-set propensity score and the marginal martingale, thereby reducing the impact of estimation bias in HTE from nuisance models including (1) marginal survival, and (2) risk-set propensity scores. This enables leveraging advances in machine learning and incorporates flexible estimators for the nuisance functions and attaining the standard optimal nonparametric estimation rate with the oracle property. Numerical experiments demonstrated empirical performance consistent with the theory. We applied the martingale R-learner to estimate the effect of alcohol on dementia using the Honolulu-Asia Aging Study data.

</details>


### [169] [Curvature-Calibrated Quasi-Bayesian Updating for Moment-Restricted Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19634)
*Masahiro Tanaka*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种曲率校准的拟贝叶斯更新方法，通过使用矩条件协方差的逆矩阵来确定权重矩阵，从而实现更准确的后验推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无法获得完整似然函数的情况下，基于矩限制的拟贝叶斯推断提供了一个灵活的基础，但传统的二次矩准则中的权重矩阵会影响矩的相对重要性和信息尺度。因此需要一种新的方法来更好地校准权重。

Method: 提出曲率校准的拟贝叶斯更新方法，利用在自洽拟后验中心处评估的矩条件的协方差（或长期协方差）的逆矩阵作为权重，并通过固定点迭代交替进行协方差估计和从固定权重的拟后验中抽样。

Result: 证明了校准映射是局部收缩的，其固定点具有一致性且达到标准参数速率；高斯近似在数据依赖权重下仍然成立，协方差为逆Godambe信息矩阵；在一致四阶矩条件下，缩放后的拟后验协方差收敛到同一矩阵。模拟结果显示几次更新后协方差校准和区间覆盖有所改善。

Conclusion: 该方法能够在不依赖参数变化权重的情况下实现良好的后验不确定性校准，在纵向二元响应数据分析中表现出色，适用于存在个体内依赖和过度识别残差矩的情形。

Abstract: Moment restrictions provide a flexible basis for quasi-Bayesian inference when a full likelihood is unavailable, but the weighting matrix in a quadratic moment criterion determines both the relative importance of the moments and the information scale of posterior updating. We propose curvature-calibrated quasi-Bayesian updating, which uses the inverse of the covariance (or long-run covariance) of the moment conditions evaluated at a self-consistent quasi-posterior center. The resulting fixed-point procedure alternates between covariance estimation and simulation from a fixed-weight quasi-posterior, thereby avoiding parameter-dependent weighting during each simulation run. Under a Bernstein-von Mises condition for the fixed-weight quasi-posterior at the efficient population weight, we show that the calibration map is locally contractive, that its fixed point is consistent at the standard parametric rate, and that the Gaussian approximation continues to hold under the calibrated data-dependent weight, with covariance given by the inverse Godambe information matrix. Under a uniform fourth-moment condition, the scaled quasi-posterior covariance converges to the same matrix, so quasi-posterior and repeated-sampling uncertainty agree to first order. Simulations show improved covariance calibration and interval coverage after a few updates. An application to longitudinal binary-response data illustrates the method with within-subject dependence and overidentified residual moments.

</details>


### [170] [Efficient Poisson Subsampling for the Partially Linear Additive Cox Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19599)
*Dongxiao Han,Liuquan Sun,Chunjie Wang,Dehui Wang,HaiYing Wang,Haixiang Zhang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出了一种高效的泊松子抽样方法，用于大规模生存数据分析中的部分线性可加Cox模型，通过B样条逼近非参数成分，并基于去相关得分技术构建估计方程，实现了无需处理完整数据集的高效统计推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大规模生存数据在计算和存储方面面临的挑战，提高分析效率并保持模型的灵活性与可解释性。

Method: 采用B样条基函数逼近非参数成分，利用去相关得分技术构建基于泊松子抽样的估计方程，并根据L最优准则确定最优子抽样概率，设计两步自适应算法以实现实际应用。

Result: 建立了估计量的渐近正态性，模拟研究和对淋巴瘤癌症数据的实际应用表明该方法在大规模设置下具有良好的效率和准确性。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够在不处理全部数据的情况下，实现对大规模生存数据的高效、准确统计推断，适用于复杂但可解释的生存模型分析。

Abstract: To address the computational and storage challenges often encountered in large-scale survival data analysis, we propose an efficient Poisson subsampling method for the partially linear additive Cox model. This model provides a flexible yet interpretable framework by incorporating linear covariate effects, additive nonparametric components for nonlinear covariates, and a nonparametric baseline hazard function. The proposed method adopts B-spline basis functions to approximate the nonparametric components and employs the decorrelated score technique to construct a Poisson subsampling-based estimation equation, based on which we establish the asymptotic normality of the resulting estimator and derive the optimal subsampling probabilities according to the L-optimality criterion. Furthermore, we design a two-step adaptive algorithm for practical implementation. The proposed approach enables computationally efficient statistical inference for large-scale survival analysis without processing the full dataset. We validate the performance of the proposed method through extensive simulation studies and a real-world application to a lymphoma cancer dataset, demonstrating its efficiency and accuracy in large-scale settings.

</details>


### [171] [Fast high-dimensional mean testing via logistic regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20286)
*Sayan Das,Debraj Das,Subhajit Dutta*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于逻辑回归的高效高维均值向量相等性检验方法，利用Lasso筛选变量并在低维下进行推断，具有良好的统计性质和计算可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维数据中检验多个总体均值向量是否相等，传统方法面临维度诅咒和分布假设限制，需要更灵活、高效且可扩展的方法。

Method: 通过将均值相等问题转化为逻辑回归参数为零的等价问题，使用逻辑Lasso筛选重要变量，并在降维后进行无偏逻辑回归重拟合以实现推断。

Result: 所提方法在渐近意义下具有正确的检验水平和一致性，在特定两样本高斯模型下达到极小极大分离速率，并可通过多类逻辑回归推广到多个总体情形；模拟显示其在不平衡设计和异方差情况下具有良好表现。

Conclusion: 该方法为高维多总体均值比较提供了有效工具，兼具理论保证和实际可扩展性，适用于如基因表达等大规模数据分析。

Abstract: We propose computationally efficient tests for equality of mean vectors of two or more high-dimensional populations. Central to our approach is an equivalence between equality of means and a zero population logistic regression parameter. We establish this equivalence for independently distributed observations without imposing common distributional assumptions across populations. Our procedure uses logistic Lasso to screen informative variables and an unpenalized logistic refit for inference in the reduced dimension, yielding asymptotically correct size and consistency. For a specified two-sample Gaussian submodel and sparse discriminative class, the test also attains the minimax separation rate. The framework extends to multiple populations through multi-class logistic regression. Simulations demonstrate accurate size control, strong power, and favorable computational scaling compared with existing tests under unbalanced designs and variance heterogeneity. Applications to gene-expression data with more than twenty-two thousand variables illustrate the practical scalability of the proposed procedures.

</details>


### [172] [Variational Goal-Oriented Optimal Experimental Design for Mixed-Distribution Quantities of Interest: Application to Ship Roll Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19631)
*Chen Cheng,Xun Huan,Yulin Pan*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于混合离散-连续量的变分目标导向最优实验设计（GO-OED）方法，通过分别建模原子概率和连续成分，解决了传统连续变分近似在处理含有原子的后验分布时导致KL散度无穷大或下界估计无效的问题，并在船舶横摇安全评估中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不确定性力学中，当存在阈值或事件驱动转换时，关心的量（QoI）可能具有混合型分布（部分离散、部分连续），传统的基于连续变分近似的EIG估计方法无法正确处理含有原子的概率结构，导致KL散度发散或下界估计失效，因此需要新的建模方法。

Method: 提出一种混合变分近似框架，将QoI的条件原子概率与连续部分分开建模，其中连续部分采用标准化流（normalizing flow）进行建模，并结合Barber-Agakov互信息下界进行EIG估计。

Result: 在解析例子中成功恢复了正确的EIG分布；在船舶横摇安全评估应用中，实现了稳定的EIG下界估计，并能识别对推断时间超越概率最有信息量的海况条件。

Conclusion: 所提出的混合变分GO-OED方法能够有效处理具有混合离散-连续特性的QoI，克服了传统连续变分方法的根本缺陷，为事件驱动型风险评估中的实验设计提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: Goal-oriented optimal experimental design (GO-OED) selects experiments according to the expected information gain (EIG) about a quantity of interest (QoI) rather than the full parameter vector. This work develops a variational GO-OED formulation for mixed discrete-continuous QoI laws arising in probabilistic mechanics when thresholding or event-based transformations map a positive-probability set of uncertain inputs to a common value while other inputs produce continuously varying responses. The motivating application is ship roll safety assessment in random waves, where the QoI is the temporal exceedance probability above a prescribed roll-angle threshold. This quantity is zero when no exceedance occurs and varies continuously over positive values otherwise. A purely continuous variational approximation does not dominate a posterior QoI law containing an atom, yielding an infinite Kullback-Leibler divergence and a trivial Barber-Agakov lower bound of $-\infty$. Scoring atom samples using continuous density values instead changes the objective and does not produce a valid lower-bound estimator. We introduce a mixed variational approximation that models the conditional atom probability and continuous component separately, with a normalizing flow used for the latter. An analytical example recovers the correct EIG landscape, while the ship roll application provides stable EIG lower-bound estimates and identifies informative wave conditions for temporal-exceedance-probability inference.

</details>


### [173] [Multivariate Spatio-Temporal Regression with Penalized Model Selection and an Empirical Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19664)
*Ryuei Nishii,Saeko Ohta,Shojiro Tanaka*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多变量一般嵌套时空（MGNST）回归框架，用于分析响应变量之间的空间、时间和跨方程依赖性，并建立了可识别性条件与惩罚似然估计方法，通过模拟和日本关西地区实证数据验证了模型在捕捉复杂依赖结构方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了统一建模多变量响应间的空间、时间及交叉依赖关系，克服现有模型在处理复杂依赖结构时的局限性。

Method: 引入包含四个参数矩阵的MGNST框架，分别刻画空间滞后依赖、空间误差依赖、时间自回归和同期误差协方差，并利用工具变量秩条件建立可识别性，采用基于有效自由度的惩罚似然法进行估计。

Result: 蒙特卡洛实验显示惩罚AIC的选择正确率随样本量和空间依赖强度增加而提高；在日本关西198个市镇的社会经济数据分析中，惩罚AIC选择了完整的MGNST模型，而惩罚BIC选择较简模型，但两者均支持时间持续性和空间误差依赖，且MGNST模型显著降低了残差中的空间自相关。

Conclusion: MGNST框架能有效识别和比较多变量时空依赖结构，为复杂空间数据建模提供了灵活且统一的方法。

Abstract: This paper develops the statistical foundations of a multivariate general nesting spatio-temporal (MGNST) regression framework for analyzing spatial, temporal, and cross-equation dependence among responses. Four parameter matrices represent spatial lag dependence, spatial error dependence,temporal autoregression, and contemporaneous error covariance. Their off-diagonal elements allow dependence to propagate within and across responses. Matrix restrictions yield eleven specifications encompassing multivariate spatial autoregressive models, multivariate spatial error models, vector autoregressive models with exogenous variables, and independent spatio-temporal regressions as special cases.
  We establish identifiability conditions using instrumental-variable rank conditions and introduce penalized likelihood estimation and information criteria based on effective degrees of freedom. Monte Carlo experiments examine three data-generating models, three sample sizes, and three levels of spatial dependence. Correct-selection rates under penalized AIC generally increase with sample size and the strength of spatial dependence, while parameter recovery improves as the sample size increases.
  For socioeconomic data from 198 municipalities in Japan's Kansai region, penalized AIC selects the full MGNST model, whereas penalized BIC selects a response-wise independent spatial error model. Despite selecting models of different complexity, both criteria support temporal persistence and spatial error dependence. The pAIC-selected MGNST model reduces strong spatial autocorrelation in the responses to negligible residual levels, demonstrating its usefulness for identifying and comparing multivariate spatio-temporal dependence structures.

</details>


### [174] [Information-Computation Inversion in Pseudo-Marginal MCMC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19718)
*Zihan Xu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在非负无偏似然估计下的伪边缘MCMC方法中，观测设计变化对后验信息和似然估计分布的影响，提出了一种新的逆权接受界，并引入路径条件转移机制以改善有限 horizon 下的后验函数恢复性能，在基因网络和Lotka-Volterra模型实验中验证了其在降低计算成本和提升恢复稳定性方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观测设计的变化不仅影响后验信息，还改变似然估计器的随机性质，导致传统伪边缘MCMC在有限迭代下难以准确恢复目标后验函数，因此需要研究如何在联合变化下实现稳定的函数恢复。

Method: 提出一类广泛的‘新鲜估计器’伪边缘MCMC框架，推导出逆权接受界以量化高权重质量下不一致状态的均方误差下界，并提出路径条件化转移机制，通过耦合辅助继承与独立刷新来优化辅助计算分配。

Result: 理论结果揭示了信息与计算之间的倒置现象：更强的后验分离可能伴随更差的有限步恢复性能；在基因网络实验中，路径条件化使粒子滤波器壁钟时间减少42.4%（95%区间39.6%-45.2%），且恢复不确定性落在预设非劣效边界内；Lotka-Volterra实验验证了该机制的可移植性。

Conclusion: 统计信息、保留状态下的函数风险与辅助计算分配在伪边缘推断中应被区分对待；路径条件化是一种有效的精确推断路径修复机制，能在复杂模型中提升计算效率与推理稳定性。

Abstract: Pseudo-marginal MCMC is exact under nonnegative unbiased likelihood estimation, but observation design can change both posterior information and the stochastic law of the likelihood estimator. We study fixed-horizon recovery of a declared posterior functional under this joint change. For a broad fresh-estimator pseudo-marginal class, we derive an inverse-weight acceptance bound that yields a lower bound on functional mean-squared error from high retained-weight mass in discrepant states. A two-region corollary establishes an information-computation inversion: stronger exact posterior separation can coexist with worse finite-horizon recovery when the estimator law changes. The obstruction motivates an exact route-conditioned transition that allocates coupled auxiliary inheritance and independent refreshment according to a scientific pair route. In a controlled transcription experiment, a finer observation strengthens likelihood separation while degrading particle-filter reliability and finite-chain behavior. In a fresh 96-state gene-network comparison, route conditioning reduces measured particle-filter wall time by 42.4% in the frozen execution (95% retained-state bootstrap interval 39.6%-45.2%). Recovery uncertainty spans the predeclared noninferiority margin. A prospective Lotka-Volterra experiment supports portability of the retained-state route-restoration mechanism. The results distinguish statistical information, retained-state functional risk, and auxiliary-computation allocation in pseudo-marginal inference.

</details>


### [175] [Causal Survival Forests with Negative Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19749)
*Zijun Gao,Kyounggeui Hong,Leyi Ma,Qianli Wu,Zachary Izzo,Ruishan Liu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于观察性生存研究中异质性处理效应（HTE）估计的新方法NC-CSF，结合因果生存森林与近端因果推断中的负控制变量，有效缓解了未观测混杂和删失带来的偏倚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在删失结果和未测量混杂因素的观察性生存研究中，准确估计异质性处理效应具有挑战性，现有方法易受偏倚影响。

Method: 将因果生存森林（CSF）与负控制（NC）相结合，提出NC-CSF方法；利用包含代理变量和Neyman正交化的损失函数训练随机森林，以减少未观测混杂导致的偏倚。

Result: 在多种模拟场景下，NC-CSF显著降低了估计偏差和误差；在多个临床数据集上验证了其有效性，复现了已有发现并揭示了新的治疗效应异质性模式。

Conclusion: NC-CSF是一种鲁棒、灵活的非参数HTE估计方法，适用于存在未测量混杂和删失的生存分析，且已提供完整的Python实现以支持实际应用。

Abstract: We study heterogeneous treatment-effect (HTE) estimation in observational survival studies commonly associated with both censored outcomes and unmeasured confounding. We integrate causal survival forests (CSF) with negative controls (NC) from proximal causal inference and introduce Negative Control Causal Survival Forests (NC-CSF), a flexible nonparametric HTE learner for survival analysis. Our approach uses a loss that incorporates proxy variables and Neyman orthogonalization to train the random forest, thereby mitigating bias from unobserved confounding and gaining robustness to nuisance estimation. Through extensive simulations spanning varying levels of confounding, proxy relevance, and censoring mechanisms, we demonstrate that NC-CSF substantially reduces bias and estimation error relative to existing baselines. We further demonstrate the practical utility of our method on various clinical datasets, where it confirms several existing findings and also reveals new interpretable patterns of treatment-effect heterogeneity. To facilitate practical use, we provide an end-to-end Python implementation of NC-CSF that carefully handles implementation details such as nuisance estimation and clipping.

</details>


### [176] [Testing the Validity of Instrumental Variable Sets in Causal Additive Models with Non-Constant Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19771)
*Xichen Guo,Feng Xie,Bingbing Tang,Yan Zeng,Zhang Hao,Zhi Geng,Ruichu Cai,Kun Zhang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于交叉辅助变量的独立性检验（CAT）条件，用于在具有非恒定效应的因果加性模型（CAM-NCE）下评估工具变量（IV）集合的有效性，并证明了该条件在特定假设下的必要性和充分性，同时开发了适用于有限样本的实用算法，通过合成数据和真实数据实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在未观测混杂因素的情况下，工具变量（IV）方法对因果效应估计非常有用，但实际中研究者常面临多个候选IV，其有效性难以从观测数据中判断。因此，需要一种可检验的条件来识别有效的IV集合。

Method: 提出了Cross Auxiliary-based independence Test (CAT) 条件，结合完备性条件和跨分布非退化条件，在CAM-NCE框架下理论分析其必要性和充分性，并扩展至含协变量的情形，进而设计了一种适用于有限样本的IV有效性检验算法。

Result: 理论上证明了在完备性条件下，若CAT条件不成立，则对应IV集无效；在额外的跨分布非退化条件下，CAT条件成为判断IV有效性的充要条件。所提算法在合成数据和三个真实世界数据集中均表现出良好的性能。

Conclusion: CAT条件为在CAM-NCE模型下识别有效IV集提供了一个可检验、理论上可靠的方法，所提出的算法具有实际应用价值，能够有效区分有效与无效的IV集合。

Abstract: Instrumental variable (IV) methods are powerful for causal effect estimation with unmeasured confounding, but in practice researchers often face a set of candidate IVs whose validity is difficult to determine from observational data. This paper studies the problem of testing the validity of IV sets under Causal Additive Models with Non-Constant Effects (CAM-NCE). To address this problem, we propose a testable condition, termed the Cross Auxiliary-based independence Test (CAT) condition, for assessing IV set validity from observational data. We show that, under the completeness condition, if the CAT condition is violated, the corresponding set cannot be a valid IV set. Furthermore, under a cross distributional non-degeneracy condition, we establish that the CAT condition becomes both necessary and sufficient for characterizing valid IV sets under CAM-NCE. We then extend the CAT condition to settings with covariates and develop a practical finite-sample algorithm for testing the validity of candidate IV sets. Extensive experiments on synthetic data and three real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness and practical utility of the proposed method.

</details>


### [177] [Distributional Extrapolation for Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19849)
*Marin Šola,Xinwei Shen,Peter Bühlmann*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DExtrI的方法，用于从有限范围的观测中外推组合效应，并提供了理论保证和实证结果，证明其在药物组合预测和超参数优化中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多科学领域（如药物发现和超参数优化）面临的一个基本挑战是如何从单变量观测数据中预测多变量组合的协同效应。

Method: 提出DExtrI方法，利用轴对齐的单协变量激活训练数据，外推多个协变量同时激活时的交互效应，并提供理论分析。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的实验表明，DExtrI能够成功泛化到未见过的协变量组合，有效预测组合效应。

Conclusion: DExtrI能够在训练数据支持范围之外实现组合交互效应的外推，具有在药物组合预测和超参数优化等应用中的潜力。

Abstract: Predicting combinatorial effects from limited-range observations is a fundamental challenge in many scientific domains, including drug discovery and hyperparameter optimization. We study combinatorial extrapolation, where training data consists of axis-aligned samples with only one active covariate, while test-time inputs involve multiple simultaneously active covariates. We introduce DExtrI, a method for extrapolating interaction effects beyond the support of the training data. We provide theoretical guarantees characterizing when such extrapolation is possible. Empirical results on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that DExtrI successfully generalizes to unseen combinations of covariates. Our approach enables applications such as predicting previously untested drug combinations and improving the efficiency of hyperparameter optimization.

</details>


### [178] [Partial Identification Learning with Categorical Treatments for Individualized Treatment Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19853)
*Johannes Hruza,Paweł Morzywołek,Jakob Zeitler,Samir Bhatt,Michael C Sachs*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于多类别治疗、结果和工具变量的个体化治疗规则（ITR）的部分识别学习框架，通过广义最小最大损失准则和对称嵌入策略，在存在未观测混杂因素的情况下优化治疗决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要局限于二元治疗设置和Balke-Pearl的因果界，难以处理多类别变量和更复杂的因果结构，因此需要扩展部分识别下的ITR优化方法。

Method: 引入广义最小最大损失准则以在多个治疗选项中选择最优治疗，并采用对称嵌入策略将离散治疗映射到正则单形顶点，构建可微加权代理损失函数并通过优化求解，同时给出满足一般正则条件下的有限样本收敛速率。

Result: 该方法在未观测混杂存在的情况下，相比现有方法能生成更接近于真实最优ITR的决策规则，数值实验验证了其优越性。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架有效扩展了部分识别下ITR的学习能力，适用于多类别变量和更广泛的因果结构，具有良好的理论保证和实际性能。

Abstract: We develop a partial identification learning framework for individualized treatment rules (ITRs) with categorical treatments, outcomes, and instrumental variables. Rather than relying on strong causal assumptions required for point identification, our framework leverages causal bounds to characterize the optimal treatment decision. Existing methods for ITR optimization under partial identification are largely restricted to binary treatment settings and the bounds derived by Balke and Pearl under the canonical instrumental variable design. We extend this framework to accommodate a broader class of causal structures as well as scenarios with categorical treatment, outcome, and instrumental variables. We introduce a generalized minimax loss criterion for treatment selection from among more than two options, which minimizes the maximum possible difference between the chosen and the optimal treatment based on partial identification bounds. To construct the ITR, we use a symmetric embedding strategy that maps discrete treatments to the vertices of a regular simplex, avoiding the geometric inconsistencies of standard one-vs-rest approaches. We derive a differentiable, weighted surrogate risk function and show that optimizing it solves the original problem. Furthermore, we provide finite sample convergence rates via an oracle inequality under general regularity conditions, which we show are satisfied by a kernel based implementation. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the framework yields ITRs significantly closer to the oracle ITR compared to existing alternatives in settings with unmeasured confounding.

</details>


### [179] [A Repeated Measurements Approach to $SoH$ Battery Modelling of Cyclic Aged Data in a Laboratory Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19879)
*Mark Cary,Charles Bokor*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种一阶线性化非线性重复测量方法，用于分析实验室条件下生成的电池老化曲线，结合正则化迭代广义最小二乘法识别分层模型，实现了对健康状态（SoH）的高精度预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更准确地反映电池老化数据中的结构特征，需要建立能够同时描述测量噪声和电池间差异的统计模型。

Method: 采用两成分方差模型：包含老化曲线内的变异（测量噪声）和老化曲线间的变异（电池间差异）。使用具有最优超参数重估计的正则化迭代广义最小二乘法进行参数识别，并用幂律表达式建模每个电池的SoH曲线，老化参数的变化通过单结点立方B样条建模。

Result: 在10个电池的SoH数据上验证了模型，SoH预测精度达到±0.191%，适用于SoH在[0,20]区间的情况。

Conclusion: 所提出的模型能有效捕捉电池老化过程中的多层次变异性，具备高预测精度，适用于受控实验条件下的电池寿命分析。

Abstract: This document describes the application of a first order linearised nonlinear repeated measurements approach to the analysis of battery cell ageing profiles generated under controlled conditions in a laboratory. The primary advantage of the model is it reflects the obvious structure in the data. Consequently, it is a two-component of variance model: variation within ageing profiles (measurement noise) and variation among ageing profiles (test-to-test or cell-to-cell) variation. Novel regularised iterative generalised least squares parameter identification schemes, with optimal hyper-parameter re-estimation, are used to identify the hierarchical nonlinear model. The training data comprised $SoH$ profiles for 10 cells aged at various constant discharge and charge current cycles at a fixed chamber environmental temperature of 25 [$^\circ$C]. Each cell $SoH$ profile is modelled using a simple power law expression, whereas the variation in ageing parameters is modelled using a single knot cubic B-spline. $SoH$ is accurately predicted to $\pm 0.191\%$ for $SOH \in [0,20]$.

</details>


### [180] [Where Does the Union Bound Go? Best-Arm Identification and Strong FWER Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19903)
*Rianne de Heide*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文讨论了固定置信度下最优臂识别中的多重检验问题，解释了为何在仅有一个真实假设的情况下仍存在K-1的Bonferroni型因子，并揭示了两种假设设定方式下多重性问题的不同表现形式及其等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在最优臂识别中，尽管只有一个臂是最优的，但证明过程中常使用对竞争臂的联合界，这引发了关于多重检验校正因子的疑问。作者旨在澄清这一表面上的矛盾。

Method: 通过从多重检验的角度分析假设设定的两种方向，将最优臂识别问题与强族-wise错误率（FWER）框架联系起来，并比较不同设定下的错误控制机制。

Result: 表明在一种设定下，存在K-1个真无效假设，属于标准FWER问题；而在另一种设定下，虽仅有一个真无效假设，但可通过K-1种比较错误地拒绝它，因此多重性问题依然存在，只是表现位置不同。

Conclusion: 最优臂识别中的多重性问题在两种假设视角下是等价的，多重性并未消失，而是以不同形式体现，本文明确了这一等价关系。

Abstract: In fixed-confidence best-arm identification, proofs often use a union bound across the competing arms. From a multiple-testing point of view this can look puzzling: if the best arm is unique, only one hypothesis of the form ``arm $i$ is best'' can be true. Why then should there be a Bonferroni-type factor of $K-1$? The answer is that there are two natural ways to orient the hypotheses. In one orientation, best-arm identification is literally a strong familywise-error-rate (FWER) problem with $K-1$ true nulls. In the opposite orientation, exactly one null is true, but a pairwise implementation can falsely reject that one null through any of $K-1$ comparisons. Thus the multiplicity has not disappeared; it just pops up in different places. This note makes the equivalence explicit in the terminology of both communities.

</details>


### [181] [Causal inference via propensity scores for case-control studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20080)
*Yan Liu,Anita Koushik,Philippe Boileau,Cong Jiang,Miceline Mésidor,Claudia Waddingham,Denis Talbot,Mireille E. Schnitzer*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在病例对照研究等结果依赖性抽样设计中使用倾向得分方法进行因果推断的问题，提出了新的估计方法和诊断工具，并通过模拟数据和实际应用（阿司匹林使用与卵巢癌风险的关系）验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 倾向得分方法在队列和实验设计中广泛应用，但在病例对照研究等结果依赖性抽样设计中的发展和应用仍有限。需要解决此类研究中混杂偏倚的统计调整问题。

Method: 利用控制组数据拟合倾向得分模型，假设控制组在协变量条件下对源人群具有代表性（“控制可交换性”）。采用逆概率加权和高效双稳健估计器，并提出一种新的两步倾向得分卡尺匹配方法。引入新的诊断工具以验证两种必要的重叠性。

Result: 证明了在三种不同的病例对照设计变体下，可以使用基于控制组拟合的倾向得分估计相对效应（如因果风险比）。通过模拟数据对比了不同估计器的表现，并成功应用于分析规律性阿司匹林使用与卵巢癌风险之间的关联。

Conclusion: 基于控制组拟合的倾向得分方法可用于病例对照研究中的因果效应估计，新提出的估计器和诊断工具提高了分析的准确性和可靠性。

Abstract: Propensity score methods for causal inference are increasingly being used in cohort and experimental designs, but their development and uptake in outcome-dependent sampling schemes, such as case-control studies, remains limited. Case-control studies involve the sampling of individuals with and without an outcome of interest with the goal of estimating the effects of past exposures. When the design is observational, statistical adjustment for confounding bias is necessary. In case-control studies, propensity score models can be fit using control data under the assumption that the controls are representative of the source population with respect to their exposure distribution conditional on covariates ("control exchangeability"). In this paper, we first demonstrate that relative effects, such as causal risk ratios, are estimable under three different case-control design variants using control-fitted propensity scores. We appropriate two existing estimators for these designs: inverse probability of treatment weighting and an efficient and doubly robust estimator. We also introduce a novel two-step propensity score caliper-matching procedure for case-control designs. We introduce novel diagnostic tools to verify two necessary types of overlap. We then contrast our estimators using simulated data and apply them to examine the association between regular aspirin use and ovarian cancer risk.

</details>


### [182] [Multi-Method Causal Evidence Synthesis: Ranking Candidate Drivers by Convergent Cross-Method Evidence from Observational Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20187)
*Manish Gupta,Dipanjan De*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了Multi-Method Causal Evidence Synthesis (MCES)框架，通过整合来自八种数学传统下的11种方法，对观测数据中的因果关系进行证据合成，生成收敛性证据评分（CES），用于优先级排序而非因果识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果推断方法多依赖单一算法，缺乏跨不同数学传统的证据整合，导致结果可能受方法假设影响。MCES旨在通过聚合多种方法的输出，提升对潜在因果关系识别的稳健性和可靠性。

Method: MCES首先应用结构-行为分解消除定义性关系，然后在面板数据上运行11种不同方法，将各方法输出归一化至[0,1]区间，并通过线性意见池（CES）进行融合，量化不同方法间的证据收敛程度。

Result: 在包含合成数据、Sachs蛋白信号网络及多个贝叶斯网络基准的实验中，MCES能将真实因果边排在前列（主场景下Precision@5=1.0，Precision@10=0.96），且极少将无关联变量对误判为中高收敛。

Conclusion: MCES提供了一种不依赖特定方法的默认选择，适用于假设优先级排序，虽不声称因果可识别性，但增强了跨方法一致性下的证据可信度。

Abstract: Practitioners inferring causality from observational data usually rely on a single method and treat its output as causal truth. Recent tools select an optimal method for a dataset, and recent ensembles aggregate multiple causal-discovery algorithms into one graph, but little work pools evidence across different mathematical traditions, including non-causal ones. We present Multi-Method Causal Evidence Synthesis (MCES), a framework that ranks which candidate drivers in an observational system are most likely relevant to a set of outcomes, and with what strength of evidence. MCES runs eleven methods across eight mathematical traditions on observational panel data and pools their outputs into a Convergent Evidence Score (CES), a linear opinion pool. CES quantifies convergence of evidence across analytical lenses: the degree to which methods with different assumptions point to the same driver-outcome relationship. It does not claim causal identification in the interventionist sense; it supports hypothesis prioritization, not a transferable probability of causation. MCES first applies Structural-Behavioral Decomposition to remove definitional (algebraic) relationships, then runs all methods, normalizes outputs to [0,1], and pools them. We distinguish MCES from method selection, structural ensembles, prediction ensembles, and literature synthesis. Using synthetic data with embedded ground truth, the Sachs protein-signaling benchmark, six Bayesian-network structure benchmarks, and two further synthetic domains, we show MCES ranks true edges near the top (Precision@5 = 1.0, Precision@10 = 0.96 on the primary scenario), with a low empirical rate of null pairs reaching Moderate-or-higher convergence. Our central point is not that the pool beats every individual method, but that no single method is uniformly best across the evaluated scenarios, so MCES offers a method-agnostic default.

</details>


### [183] [A Bayesian Edge-Space Framework for Whole-Connectome Inference in Multisite Autism Neuroimaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20243)
*Montserrat Fuentes,Veronica B. Patterson*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出了一种贝叶斯边空间回归框架，用于在考虑解剖和功能相似性的同时建模自闭症谱系障碍（ASD）对全脑连接组的影响，揭示了ASD相关连接的异质性重组模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASD与大脑多个系统的异质性改变有关，传统的全连接组推断方法因连接数量多且存在依赖关系而面临挑战。

Method: 开发了一种贝叶斯边空间回归模型，将每个参与者的连接组视为网络值响应，通过正定协方差结构直接在连接上建模，并利用对称化端点匹配操作将解剖和功能相似性从区域提升到边空间，结合低秩核表示和充分统计量简化实现全连接组计算。

Result: 该方法在模拟中表现出对效应面（尤其是弱信号下）更优的恢复能力；在ABIDE数据中识别出广泛的ASD相关连接减少及局部增加，支持分布式神经系统的异质性重组而非统一的过度或低连接。

Conclusion: ASD相关的脑连接变化呈现复杂且非均匀的模式，功能组织在连接变异中起主导作用，超越了解剖邻近性的影响。

Abstract: Autism spectrum disorder (ASD) is associated with heterogeneous alterations across distributed brain systems, creating challenges for whole-connectome inference. The difficulty arises not only from the large number of connections, but also from dependence among effects indexed by anatomically and functionally related region pairs. We introduce a Bayesian Edge-Space regression framework that treats each participant's connectome as a network-valued response and models the adjusted ASD effect over unordered brain-region pairs. The main methodological contribution is a positive-semidefinite covariance construction defined directly on connections. Anatomical and diagnosis-blind functional similarities are lifted from regions to edge space through a symmetrized endpoint-matching operation that preserves endpoint identity and is invariant to endpoint ordering. An additive Bayesian hierarchy estimates anatomical, functional, and interaction contributions together with multisite adjustments and connection-specific effects. Theoretical results establish covariance validity and continuous nesting of the structured components. Low-rank kernel representations and an exact sufficient-statistic reduction enable whole-connectome computation without preliminary edgewise estimation. Simulations show improved recovery of the effect surface, particularly under weak signals. In the Autism Brain Imaging Data Exchange, the framework identifies widespread reductions together with localized increases in ASD-associated connectivity. This pattern supports heterogeneous reorganization across distributed neural systems rather than uniform hyper- or hypoconnectivity. Under the fitted parameterization, the functional component has the largest structural scale, indicating organization beyond anatomical proximity alone.

</details>


### [184] [GENIE: Generative Neural Inference for Epidemics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20253)
*Laura M. Guzmán-Rincón,George R. E. Bradley,Joel Kandiah,Kyriakos Flouris,Pietro Liò,Paul J. Birrell,Alexander E. Zarebski,Daniela De Angelis*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为GENIE的生成神经推断框架，用于高分辨率呼吸道病原体传播预测，结合了跨区域共享机制与本地特征，通过机器学习加速基于复杂代理模型的近实时疫情预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统传染病预测模型在细粒度空间分辨率下难以准确捕捉复杂的传播动态，而高精度的基于代理的模型（ABM）又计算成本过高，无法实时使用，因此需要一种兼顾准确性与效率的新方法。

Method: 提出GENIE框架，包含两个模块：局部感染编码器（学习跨区域共享的疾病传播动态）和本地特征编码器（学习各地区的特定影响因素），利用高分辨率ABM模拟数据训练该模型，以生成未来疫情轨迹的近似后验预测分布。

Result: 在多种评估指标上，GENIE优于传统的统计和机器学习模型，尤其在预测住院人数峰值的时间和规模方面表现更优。

Conclusion: GENIE通过结合共享生物学机制与地区特异性特征，实现了高分辨率、近实时的流行病负担预测，为复杂疫情建模提供了高效可行的解决方案。

Abstract: The SARS-CoV-2 pandemic highlighted the ongoing risk infectious diseases pose to society and the value of reliable information on the likely future burden. When forecasting an epidemic at fine spatial resolution, traditionally used mechanistic compartmental model struggle to capture highly complex granular transmission dynamics, resulting in inaccurate and overconfident forecasts. However, detailed Agent-Based Models (ABMs), are challenging to calibrate and are too computationally expensive to use in real-time. Amortized simulation-based inference promises to overcome this difficulty by exploiting the power of machine learning (ML) to perform approximate forecasting at near-real-time using arbitrarily complex models of epidemics. In this work we introduce Generative Neural Inference for Epidemics (GENIE), a spatio-temporal ML-based framework for high-resolution forecasting of the burden of respiratory pathogens. GENIE is designed to reflect two key characteristics of outbreaks: (i) shared biological mechanisms across locations and (ii) location-specific characteristics affecting transmission dynamics. This results in the model architecture having two modules: (i) a Local Infection Encoder - which learns to represent disease dynamics shared across all locations and (ii) a Local Profile Encoder - which learns location-specific representations. Using simulations from a high-resolution spatio-temporal ABM, GENIE is trained to generate samples from an approximate posterior predictive distribution of future epidemic trajectories. Benchmarked against established statistical and ML models, GENIE demonstrates superior performance across a range of measures including the timing and magnitude of peak hospitalisations.

</details>


### [185] [From Kriging to Spatial AI: Fifty Years of Spatial Statistics for Complex Dependent Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20260)
*Montserrat Fuentes,Veronica B. Patterson*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文综述了空间统计从克里金法到贝叶斯层次模型和可扩展计算的发展，并探讨其与空间人工智能（Spatial AI）的融合，强调利用依赖性学习、保持可解释结构，并结合空间几何与物理知识以实现科学上有意义的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着空间数据日益复杂，传统方法已不足以应对，需将空间统计与机器学习相结合，发展更具解释性和灵活性的新方法。

Method: 回顾空间统计的发展历程，包括随机场、谱方法、贝叶斯层次模型和可扩展计算，并引入图学习与神经网络在空间数据中的应用，提出统一框架连接经典方法与Spatial AI。

Result: 建立了空间统计与Spatial AI之间的连续性，展示了如何通过融合统计建模与深度学习来处理空间依赖数据，同时保留可解释性并提升预测能力。

Conclusion: 空间统计与机器学习并非孤立发展，而是共同演进；未来方向应是结合领域知识、几何结构与灵活表示学习，推动科学驱动的空间智能发展。

Abstract: Spatial statistics has grown from kriging for spatial prediction into a broad framework for learning from complex dependent data. This article traces that development from random fields and spectral methods to Bayesian hierarchical models and scalable computation. It then connects these foundations to Spatial AI, where graph learning and neural networks are being adapted to spatially dependent data. The article introduces the main ideas behind kriging and nonstationarity and explains how data fusion and uncertainty quantification extend spatial inference to more complex settings. The central contribution is a unified account of how these developments lead naturally to new forms of Spatial AI. Rather than treating spatial statistics and machine learning as separate traditions, we show how both learn from dependence while preserving interpretable structure. We also examine how spatial geometry and physical knowledge can guide flexible representation learning and support scientifically meaningful prediction.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [186] [Tau-Rho Equality and Other Dependence Measures of a Subclass of Factorizable Copulas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19608)
*Noppawit Yanpaisan,Tippawan Santiwipanont,Matthias Scherer,Songkiat Sumetkijakan*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了Kendall's tau和Spearman's rho在某些可分解copula族中相等的条件，证明了由分段线性单调满射生成的copula满足τ=ρ，并进一步分析了Chatterjee相关系数和尾部相依性等性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于Kendall's tau和Spearman's rho在实际应用中常被视为可互换，但可能差异显著，因此探究它们何时相等具有理论与应用价值。

Method: 通过构造形如Ce,α∗Cβ,e的可分解copula，其中α和β为分段线性单调满射（PLMS），并在此类copula上推导和验证各类相依性度量的关系。

Result: 证明了所有此类PLMS生成的copula均满足τC=ρC；得到了Chatterjee系数和尾部相依系数的代数表达式，并发现Chatterjee系数可能存在极端不对称现象。

Conclusion: 在PLMS生成的可分解copula族中，Kendall's tau与Spearman's rho恒等，这为理解两种相关性度量的一致性提供了新视角，同时揭示了其他依赖结构的复杂行为。

Abstract: Kendall's tau and Spearman's rho, two widely used dependence measures in statistics and risk management, are often treated as interchangeable, yet can disagree sharply: Schreyer et al.~(2017) established the exact region of attainable $(τ,ρ)$ pairs. We study the complementary question of equality, namely, identifying nontrivial families of copulas $C$ satisfying $τ_C=ρ_C$. We prove that this equality holds for every factorizable copula of the form $C_{e,α}\ast C_{β,e}$, where $α$ and $β$ are piecewise linear monotonic surjections (PLMS). For these PLMS-generated copulas, we study further dependence measures, including Chatterjee's rank correlation coefficient and tail dependence coefficients, revealing some useful algebraic formulas and unexpected phenomena. In particular, Chatterjee's coefficient can exhibit extreme asymmetry.

</details>


### [187] [Trustworthy Decisions in Reliability Set Estimation under Insufficient Model Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19815)
*Holger Dette,Zhengfu Liu,Jun Yu*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一框架，通过建模-校准流程和共形风险控制，在存在模型误设的情况下实现对可靠性集的可信决策，并通过自适应设计提高关键区域的估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于工作模型对真实响应面的近似不完美，直接依赖该模型进行可靠性集估计可能导致决策风险，误将不安全区域判断为安全区域。因此需要一种能够降低此类风险的可信决策方法。

Method: 采用建模-校准两步法：首先使用独立校准集选择偏差校正阈值，以减少决策风险；其次利用基于替代损失的共形风险控制来控制最严重的错误——错误包含风险；最后通过自适应设计集中观测于可靠性集及其边界以提升模型质量。

Result: 所提方法实现了O_P(1/n)的体积收敛速度，能够在有限样本下控制错误包含风险至预设水平，且不依赖于工作模型的质量，数值实验表明其相比传统插件方法具有更准确的集合估计和更好的风险控制能力。

Conclusion: 分离的校准集对于实现可靠的风险控制是必要的，结合校准策略与自适应采样可有效提升安全性关键决策中的可靠性集估计性能。

Abstract: Reliability set estimation identifies input regions where a response probability exceeds a target level, bridging estimation and safety-critical decisions. Practitioners typically start with a working model, an imperfect approximation of the true response surface. Relying on this imperfect model may incur decision risk, potentially certifying unsafe regions as safe. We develop a unified framework that turns such a working model into a trustworthy decision rule. First, a modeling-then-calibration procedure decouples estimation from decision. Since the true set is unobservable, we introduce an asymmetric, observable surrogate loss and use a separate calibration set to select a bias-correcting threshold, reducing decision risk and achieving $O_P(1/n)$ volume convergence. Second, we leverage conformal risk control with the surrogate loss to control false inclusion risk, which is the most safety-critical error, at a pre-specified level regardless of working model quality. Together, these calibration procedures show that a separate calibration set is necessary for risk control. Third, an adaptive design concentrates observations on the reliability set and its boundary, improving model quality where errors most affect decisions while controlling budget elsewhere. Numerical studies show not only more accurate set estimates but also calibrated finite-sample risk control that classical plug-in methods lack.

</details>


### [188] [Combining Concurrent and Historical Functional Linear Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19874)
*Alois Kneip,Dominik Liebl,Sven Otto*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种函数对函数的线性回归模型，其中时间t的响应依赖于预测变量的过去轨迹及其同时刻值。通过正交化表示方法，提出了两种系数函数的样条估计量，并建立了相容性速率，揭示了路径正则性与特征值衰减之间的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决函数对函数回归模型中并发效应和历史效应不可识别的问题，明确区分这两种效应以提高模型解释性和准确性。

Method: 利用预测变量过程的正交化表示，提出平滑样条估计方法来估计并发和历史效应的系数函数，并分析其一致性速率。

Result: 发现当预测变量协方差算子的特征函数不逐点平方可和时，两种效应可以被分别识别；估计量的一致性速率显示路径越平滑，历史效应估计收敛越快，而路径越粗糙，并发效应估计收敛越快。

Conclusion: 所提出的模型和估计方法能够有效分离并发与历史效应，且在模拟和实际数据中表现出良好的性能，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: We study a function-on-function linear regression model in which the response at time $t$ depends on both the past trajectory of a predictor and its concurrent value. The model combines an $L^2$-historical effect with a point-evaluation effect, and these two coefficient functions are not automatically identifiable. We characterize the resulting non-identifiability and show that the concurrent and historical effects are separately identifiable whenever the covariance eigenfunctions of the covariance operator of the predictor are not pointwise square-summable. This mild and novel condition prevents the concurrent point evaluation from being represented by an $L^2$-historical effect.
  Building on an orthogonalized representation of the predictor process, we propose a smoothing-spline estimator for both coefficient functions and establish consistency rates. The rates reveal an interesting trade-off between path regularity and eigenvalue decay: smoother predictor trajectories lead to faster convergence of the historical-effect estimator, whereas rougher trajectories lead to faster convergence of the concurrent-effect estimator. Simulation studies and two real-data applications demonstrate the practical importance of disentangling concurrent and historical effects.

</details>


### [189] [The exact Spearman rho-footrule region via optimal transport with applications to finite rankings, mixability, and Chatterjee's rank correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20176)
*Jonathan Ansari,Marcus Rockel*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文解决了在给定Spearman脚规（footrule）的情况下，确定Spearman相关系数ρ的最大值这一开放性问题，完整刻画了这两个统计量的可达区域。通过将原问题转化为带有线性矩约束的最优传输问题，并构造唯一的最优耦合，实现了该目标。该结果在排序不等式、广义可混合性及Chatterjee相关性与秩相关比的关系等方面具有多个应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Spearman's rho与Spearman's footrule之间精确可达区域的开放问题，填补两者关系的理论空白。

Method: 将基于copula的优化问题重新表述为带有线性矩约束的最优运输问题，构造唯一的最优耦合；通过可行的对偶势函数及其接触集进行证明。等价地，该耦合在所有具有给定期望E|U-V|的均匀变量耦合中最小化|U-V|的方差。

Result: 完全确定了Spearman's rho在给定Spearman's footrule下的最大值；构造出唯一的最优耦合；得到了有限排序中的改进型Cauchy-Schwarz不等式；刻画了广义可混合性下的常数分布特性并求得最小二次偏差；推导出Chatterjee相关系数ξ(X,Y)与秩相关比之间的显式界限。

Conclusion: 本文通过最优传输方法彻底解决了Spearman相关系数与脚规之间的极值关系问题，提供了统一的理论框架，并在排序分析、相关性度量和统计不等式等多个领域展现出广泛应用价值。

Abstract: We solve the open problem of determining the maximal value of Spearman's rho when Spearman's footrule is prescribed, thereby completing the exact attainable region of these two quantities. To prove this result, we reformulate the underlying copula optimization problem as an optimal transport problem with a linear moment constraint and construct the unique optimal coupling through a matching feasible dual potential and its contact set. Equivalently, this coupling minimizes the variance of $|U-V|$ among all couplings of $U,V\sim\mathcal{U}(0,1)$ with prescribed mean $\mathbb{E}|U-V|$. Our main result admits several applications: First, in the context of finite rankings, we obtain an improved Cauchy--Schwarz inequality between Spearman's footrule distance and the associated quadratic rank difference. Second, in the framework of generalized mixability, we characterize the attainable constant values of $|U'+V'|$, for $U',V'\sim\mathcal{U}(-1/2,1/2)$, and determine the minimal quadratic deviation for a given mean. Third, we derive explicit bounds relating Chatterjee's rank correlation $ξ(X,Y)$, which can detect complex functional dependence of $Y$ on $X$, to the copula correlation ratio--a rank-based fraction of explained variance--by exploiting their conditional i.i.d. representations in terms of Spearman's footrule and Spearman's rho.

</details>


### [190] [Self-Normalizing Denominators in Rational Causal Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20223)
*Shu Tamano*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该论文研究了线性结构方程模型中因果估计量的分母行为，发现某些协方差多项式分母在高斯抽样下具有一阶不变性，并提出精确的Wishart枢轴方法；在二维完全成立，三维大部分成立，且前门调整的Wald推断即使在中介残差方差趋近于零时仍保持有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解线性结构方程模型中因果估计量的弱识别问题，尤其是分母趋近于零时的统计行为，以改进推断的稳健性和可靠性。

Method: 利用协方差多项式和嵌套协方差子式的幂积性质，结合高斯抽样下的分布理论，构建精确的Wishart枢轴，并通过因子与秩准则分类分母形式。

Result: 证明了某些分母在一阶上不能趋近于零，其标准化值在每一样本中恒定；在二维情形下结论完备，三维中除一个混合族外均被分类；前门调整的Wald推断在残差方差趋零时仍有效；模拟显示前门覆盖率为标称水平，而近似工具变量方法覆盖率下降。

Conclusion: 某些因果估计量的分母具有内在稳定性，不应简单以大小判断识别强度；应使用与分母相关的诊断而非朴素诊断来评估模型有效性。

Abstract: Rational causal estimators in linear structural equation models take the form of one covariance polynomial divided by another, and a small denominator is commonly interpreted as weak identification. We show that, under Gaussian sampling, some denominators cannot enter this regime at first order. Their sampling variation is exactly proportional to their magnitude, so the standardized denominator is constant in every sample. Products of powers of nested covariance minors have this property in every dimension and admit an exact Wishart pivot. The converse is complete in dimension two. In dimension three, one mixed family remains open, while a factor-and-rank criterion classifies all denominators with linear or quadratic determinant-free factors and covers instrumental-variable, front-door and proximal formulas. For linear front-door adjustment, Wald inference remains asymptotically valid even as the mediator residual variance vanishes at an arbitrary rate, provided the treatment--mediator coefficient is nonzero. In simulations, proximal Wald coverage fell as a naive treatment--proxy diagnostic strengthened, while front-door coverage stayed nominal, and right-heart-catheterization data distinguished naive from denominator-relevant diagnostics.

</details>


### [191] [Large Sample Properties of Higher Order Markov Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20321)
*Tuhin Majumder,Donald E. K. Martin,Soumendra N. Lahiri*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 研究了当高阶马尔可夫链的阶数随序列长度增长时的大样本性质，建立了在自然遍历性和稀疏性条件下的中心极限定理，并将其应用于二元变长马尔可夫链，为稀疏或分区的高阶模型提供了渐近推断基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了理解高阶马尔可夫链在阶数随样本量增长时的行为，特别是在稀疏和高维情况下的统计推断问题。

Method: 通过将过程嵌入到一个一阶马尔可夫链中，并利用返回时间分解技术，在适当的遍历性和稀疏性条件下证明了加性泛函的中心极限定理。

Result: 得到了适用于三角阵列且允许阶数趋于无穷而阶数与样本量之比趋于零的中心极限定理；对二元变长马尔可夫链给出了平稳质量的显式下界及保证CLT成立的具体增长速率条件。

Conclusion: 该工作为包括变长马尔可夫链和稀疏马尔可夫模型在内的高阶稀疏模型提供了坚实的渐近理论基础，支持更大一类模型的有效统计推断。

Abstract: We study large-sample properties of higher-order Markov chains on a finite alphabet $Σ$ when the order $m_n$ is allowed to grow with the sequence length $n$. By embedding the process into a first-order chain on $Σ^{m_n}$ and exploiting return-time decompositions, we establish a central limit theorem for additive functionals $\sum_{t}\! g_n(Y_t^{(n)})$ under natural ergodicity and sparsity conditions. The normalization involves the stationary return time to a suitably chosen state and accommodates triangular arrays with $m_n\!\to\!\infty$ and $m_n/n\!\to\!0$. We further illustrate the assumptions in a binary variable length Markov chain (VLMC), deriving explicit lower bounds on stationary masses that yield a concrete growth regime (e.g., $m_n\log m_n/n \to 0$) ensuring the CLT. These results provide asymptotic foundations for inference in sparse/partitioned higher-order models; including VLMCs and sparse Markov models (SMMs) where the effective dimensionality grows with the sample size.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [192] [Exact expressions of correlation functions between two spins in the boundary row of the two-dimensional rectangular Ising model with periodic-free boundary conditions and finite size](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19274)
*Mei Tao*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了二维矩形Ising模型在周期-自由边界条件下有限尺寸时边界行中两个自旋间关联函数的精确表达式，并讨论了这些表达式的性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了理解有限尺寸和不同边界条件下Ising模型中自旋关联行为，尤其是在热力学极限下的表现。

Method: 推导出关联函数的精确解析表达式，并分析在不同极限顺序下L和N参数的影响，同时与前人结果进行对比验证。

Result: 得到了边界自旋关联函数的精确表达式；显示了随着温度降低出现长程序的现象；发现热力学极限下结果依赖于L和N的取极限顺序；并通过图示说明了不同尺寸对关联函数的影响；证明本文结果在热力学极限下与前人结果形式一致。

Conclusion: 本文的精确解清晰展示了Ising模型的长程有序特性，且在热力学极限下与已有研究一致，验证了方法的正确性。

Abstract: We present exact expressions of correlation functions between two spins in the boundary row of the two-dimensional rectangular Ising model with periodic-free boundary conditions and finite size. Some properties of exact expressions obtained are discussed. The fundamental property of the Ising model that the long range order emerge as the temperature decrease is shown clearly; expressions of the correlation functions in the thermodynamic limit varies depending on the order of taking limits of two parameters L and N; the impact of different sizes on correlation functions is illustrated with the aid of diagrams. Expressions of the correlation function discussed in this paper in the thermodynamic limit has been presented by previous researchers, and we prove that expressions obtained in this article is identical in form to expressions provided by previous researchers in the thermodynamic limit.

</details>


### [193] [Quantum Rényi-Jarzynski Equality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19320)
*Benjamin Bobell,Mert Okyay,Rahul Nandkishore*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出了基于资源理论的非破坏性量子Jarzynski等式，通过Rényi-k散度量化有限热库在非绝热驱动下的偏离平衡行为，并可作为量子最优控制中抑制热库扰动的可调成本函数，揭示了Rényi阶数对分布区域敏感性的调节作用及系统-热库纠缠在最小化扰动中的关键角色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子形式的Jarzynski等式依赖于破坏相干性的测量协议，限制了其在量子系统中的应用，因此需要发展一种非破坏性的、适用于量子资源理论框架的新等式。

Method: 采用资源理论方法，推导出基于任意热库可观测量结果条件下的非破坏性量子Jarzynski等式，并引入Rényi-k散度来刻画热库在非绝热驱动下的非平衡演化。

Result: 得到了Rényi-Jarzynski等式，可用于量化有限热库的平衡偏离；该等式可作为量子最优控制中抑制热库扰动（如态制备和门设计）的成本函数；模型显示存在临界k值导致不同极小值间的转变；当驱动参数随热库能级变化时，最小化扰动需产生系统-热库纠缠。

Conclusion: Rényi-Jarzynski等式为理解和控制开放量子系统中的非平衡过程提供了新工具，且通过调节Rényi阶数可优化控制策略，同时揭示了纠缠在抑制交叉干扰中的必要性。

Abstract: The Jarzynski equality provides a strict link between nonequilibrium work and equilibrium free energy changes. Its typical quantum formulations, however, rely on measurement protocols that destroy coherence. In this Letter, we use the resource-theoretic approach to derive a non-destructive quantum Jarzynski equality conditioned on the outcomes of an arbitrary bath observable. This yields the Rényi-Jarzynski equality, which quantifies a finite bath's drift from equilibrium under a non-adiabatic drive via the Rényi $k$-divergence. We further demonstrate that the Rényi-Jarzynski equality provides a tunable cost function for quantum optimal control problems where minimizing bath drift is desired, such as state preparation and gate design, enabling the minimization of cross-talk in finite quantum systems. Our toy model exhibits a transition between competing minima for some critical value of $k$, illustrating how the Rényi order tunes sensitivity to different regions of a bath distribution. Strikingly, when drive parameters vary across bath energy levels, minimizing bath drift requires generating system-bath entanglement.

</details>


### [194] [The structure of networks that evolve under a combination of growth, via node addition and random attachment, and contraction, via random node deletion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2209.10027)
*Barak Budnick,Ofer Biham,Eytan Katzav*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过节点增加和随机连接（增长）与随机节点删除（收缩）共同作用下网络演化的结构特性，利用主方程方法得到了随时间变化的度分布解析表达式，并分析了不同增长-收缩平衡参数η下的稳态度分布形式及收敛行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解在同时存在增长和收缩过程的动态网络中，结构（特别是度分布）如何演化，填补纯增长模型无法描述现实网络收缩现象的空白。

Method: 采用基于主方程的方法，对具有概率P_add的节点添加与随机连接、以及概率P_del的随机节点删除的网络演化模型进行解析求解，引入参数η = P_add - P_del刻画增长与收缩的平衡。

Result: 得到了时间依赖的度分布P_t(k)的闭式表达式，包含依赖初值且随时间衰减的项和一个稳态分布P_st(k)；当η=1时P_st(k)为指数分布，当-1<η<1时为类泊松分布且具类泊松尾部；在网络总体增长（0<η<1）时P_t(k)最终收敛至P_st(k)；在网络收缩情况下，当-1/3<η<0时快速收敛，而当-1<η<-1/3时收敛极慢，仅在网络消失前才接近P_st(k)。

Conclusion: 网络的增长与收缩动态显著影响其最终结构特征，尤其是度分布的形式与收敛速度，揭示了现实网络在规模变化过程中结构稳健性与临界行为的存在。

Abstract: We present analytical results for the emerging structure of networks that evolve via a combination of growth (by node addition and random attachment) and contraction (by random node deletion). To this end we consider a network model in which at each time step a node addition and random attachment step takes place with probability $P_{add}$ and a random node deletion step takes place with probability $P_{del}=1-P_{add}$. The balance between the growth and contraction processes is captured by the parameter $η=P_{add}-P_{del}$. The case of pure network growth is described by $η=1$. In case that $0<η<1$ the rate of node addition exceeds the rate of node deletion and the overall process is of network growth. In the opposite case, where $-1<η<0$, the overall process is of network contraction, while in the special case of $η=0$ the expected size of the network remains fixed, apart from fluctuations. Using the master equation we obtain a closed form expression for the time dependent degree distribution $P_t(k)$. The degree distribution $P_t(k)$ includes a term that depends on the initial degree distribution $P_0(k)$, which decays as time evolves, and an asymptotic distribution $P_{st}(k)$. In the case of pure network growth ($η=1$) the asymptotic distribution $P_{st}(k)$ follows an exponential distribution, while for $-1<η<1$ it consists of a sum of Poisson-like terms and exhibits a Poisson-like tail. In the case of overall network growth ($0 < η< 1$) the degree distribution $P_t(k)$ eventually converges to $P_{st}(k)$. In the case of overall network contraction ($-1 < η< 0$) we identify two different regimes. For $-1/3 < η< 0$ the degree distribution $P_t(k)$ quickly converges towards $P_{st}(k)$. In contrast, for $-1 < η< -1/3$ the convergence of $P_t(k)$ is initially very slow and it gets closer to $P_{st}(k)$ only shortly before the network vanishes.

</details>


### [195] [State convertibility and fluctuation theorems from a dynamical reference: majorization meets martingales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19391)
*Davide Cugini,Giacomo Guarnieri*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于任意参考分布$g(t)$的$g(t)$-majorization概念，并将其与马尔可夫过程联系起来，导出了参考相对熵产生的精确涨落定理，可用于检测参考演化模型的误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在热力学中，状态转换性通常依赖于固定的参考态（如微正则或正则系综），但实际系统可能涉及时变或非标准参考分布，因此需要推广现有的majorization和thermo-majorization框架以适应更一般的场景。

Method: 通过引入$g(t)$-majorization统一并扩展了传统majorization概念，结合Lorenz曲线分析；进一步提出对偶视角，将状态转换问题转化为一维凸序问题，并证明其与马尔可夫过程的关系；基于此推导出关于相对熵产生的涨落定理，并利用$χ^2$-散度建立对参考误差的下界估计。

Result: 1) 建立了适用于任意（含时）参考分布$g(t)$的$g(t)$-majorization理论；2) 发现状态可转化当且仅当相对布居数$k_j(t)/g_j(t)$构成一个鞅；3) 导出精确的参考相对熵产生涨落定理；4) 提出一种无需先验知识的模型无关诊断方法，可用观测到的涨落关系破坏来定量估计参考模型误差。

Conclusion: 该工作为非平衡热力学中的状态转换提供了新的数学框架和物理洞察，所提出的$g(t)$-majorization和鞅条件揭示了资源理论中动态参考态的作用，而基于涨落定理的误差认证方法具有实验应用潜力。

Abstract: State convertibility represents a fundamental concept used to determine whether a transformation is possible given a specific set of resources. Within the field of Thermodynamics, where physical process are required to preserve a reference state typically in microcanonical or canonical form, this translates into the notions of majorization and thermo-majorization ---criteria that require constructing and comparing state-dependent Lorenz curves. In this work, we firstly unify and extend these notions to an arbitrary and possibly time-dependent reference distribution $g(t)$, introducing the concept of $g(t)$-majorization; we then introduce a dual picture whereby state convertibility is turned into a one-dimensional convex-order problem, which allows us to demonstrate that a transition is admissible if and only if the associated real-valued distributions of relative populations $ k_j(t)/g_j(t)$ are connected by a martingale. Building on it, we then derive an exact fluctuation theorem for a reference-relative entropy production whose average violation certifies, through a $χ^{2}$-divergence bound, the mismatch between an assumed and the true reference evolution---a model-independent diagnostic that requires no independent characterization of the latter and turns an observed breakdown of the fluctuation relation into a certified lower bound on the reference error.

</details>


### [196] [Statistical complexity from fluctuations in the information content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19485)
*Renio S. Mendes,Sergio Picoli,Evaldo M. F. Curado*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出信息内容的方差（$C$）可作为统计复杂性的自然度量，满足有序和等概率状态时为零、在中间态达到最大值等标准，并与热力学、相变等现象直接关联，且在二维伊辛模型等系统中表现出对连续相变的最大响应，适用于混沌映射和分数高斯噪声等关联系统的结构识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找一个既能反映系统非平凡结构又满足统计复杂性基本准则的普适数量，并建立其与热力学及相变之间的理论联系。

Method: 基于信息论中的信息内容方差（$C$）进行理论分析，验证其在有序态、无序态及临界相变点的行为，并通过二维伊辛模型、混沌映射和分数高斯噪声等实例进行数值验证。

Result: $C$ 在有序和完全随机状态下趋近于零，在中间复杂状态达到峰值，具有热力学广延性，正比于能量涨落和热容，并在连续相变点出现最大值，能有效捕捉多种相关系统的动力学结构特征。

Conclusion: 信息内容的方差 $C$ 是一种合理的统计复杂性度量，兼具理论一致性与实际适用性，能够连接信息论、统计物理与复杂系统行为。

Abstract: We argue that the variance of the information content ($C$), an information-theoretic quantity, can be naturally interpreted as a measure of statistical complexity. We show that $C$ satisfies widely accepted criteria for statistical complexity measures: it vanishes for both ordered and equiprobable states, while attaining maxima in intermediate regimes, typically shifted toward order. This interpretation establishes direct connections with thermodynamics and phase transitions: for systems obeying Boltzmann--Gibbs statistics, $C$ is extensive and directly proportional to energy fluctuations and heat capacity. Moreover, unlike other statistical complexity measures, it attains a maximum at continuous phase transitions, as illustrated for the two-dimensional Ising model. Applications to chaotic maps and fractional Gaussian noise further indicate that $C$ captures nontrivial dynamical structure in different classes of correlated systems.

</details>


### [197] [Exact partition function of arithmetic Ising model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19605)
*Anu Dhochak,Ken Kikuchi,Shrinit Singh*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出了d维算术伊辛模型（AIM）的精确配分函数的紧凑公式，针对2×2系统，利用q-赫维茨-勒奇ζ函数解析表达，并推导出自由能和熵的显式形式，发现高温下熵增加，支持存在熵序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了精确描述d维算术伊辛模型的热力学性质，特别是熵行为，研究者试图找到一个紧凑且可解析处理的配分函数表达式。

Method: 采用q-赫维茨-勒奇ζ函数对2×2系统的配分函数进行解析表达，并由此推导自由能和熵的具体形式。

Result: 得到了d维算术伊辛模型的精确配分函数公式；在2×2情况下，给出了自由能和熵的显式结果；发现高温时熵随温度升高而增加。

Conclusion: 高温下熵的增加表明系统中存在由熵驱动的有序现象，即“熵序”，这为理解算术伊辛模型中的相变机制提供了新视角。

Abstract: We present a compact formula for the exact partition function of the $d$-dimensional arithmetic Ising model (AIM). For a $2\times2$ system, we express it analytically using the $q$-Hurwitz-Lerch zeta function and derive explicit forms for the free energy and entropy. Additionally, we find that the entropy increases at high temperatures, supporting the presence of entropic order.

</details>


### [198] [Splitting probabilities for Brownian motion with diffusing boundaries: Application to polymer translocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20226)
*Alexander K. Hartmann,Satya N. Majumdar,Alberto Rosso*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究了由于聚合-解聚过程导致链长随机波动的聚合物链通过纳米孔的易位过程，将其映射为带有两个扩散汇的随机行走模型，并精确计算了分裂概率和易位时间及链长的概率分布，发现其具有依赖于扩散系数的幂律尾部特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解在链长动态变化情况下聚合物链通过纳米孔的易位动力学行为。

Method: 将问题映射为一个带两个扩散汇的随机行走模型，利用解析方法求解分裂概率以及易位时间和链长的概率分布，并通过数值模拟验证结果。

Result: 得到了分裂概率的精确表达式，发现其比固定链长情况更为复杂；获得了易位时间和完成时链长的概率分布，二者均呈现幂律尾部，指数连续依赖于D1、D2和D3；并通过数值模拟验证了解析结果。此外还研究了孔径进行分数布朗运动时的情形。

Conclusion: 链长的动态波动显著影响聚合物易位过程的动力学特性，扩散汇的移动性导致分裂概率和时间分布出现非平凡的幂律行为。

Abstract: We study the translocation of a polymer chain through a nanopore where the chain length fluctuates stochastically due to the polymerization-depolymerization processes at the chain ends. We map this process to an equivalent representation where the pore performs a stochastic random-walk-like process on a line in the presence of two diffusing sinks on either side of it with diffusion constants $D_1$ and $D_3$ respectively. The translocation process terminates when the pore hits either of the two outer diffusing sinks. In the case where the pore motion itself is diffusive with diffusion constant $D_2$, we compute exactly the splitting probability that the pore hits the left (right) sink before hitting the right (left) sink. We show that the splitting probability in the presence of mobile sinks is rather nontrivial compared to the classical case of immobile sinks (the latter corresponds to the case when the chain length is fixed). Furthermore, we also compute exactly the probability distribution of the translocation time and that of the chain length at the completion time of the translocation. We show that both distributions have power law tails with exponents that depend continuously on the diffusion constants $D_1$, $D_2$ and $D_3$. We validate our analytical predictions via numerical simulations. We then present numerical results for the case when the pore performs a fractional Brownian motion with Hurst exponent $0<H<1$, while the sinks are still diffusive.

</details>


### [199] [Exact Fluctuation-Response Relations for Underdamped Langevin Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20013)
*Tan Van Vu,Van Tuan Vo,Ruicheng Bao,Keiji Saito*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文推导了适用于任意时间依赖驱动和广义可加观测量的欠阻尼朗之万动力学的精确有限时间涨落-响应等式，揭示了涨落、响应与耗散之间的基本联系，并提出了基于摩擦响应的热力学不确定性关系，能够饱和且适用于任意有限观测时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在欠阻尼动力学中，传统的平均电流不确定性关系可能失效，涨落、响应与耗散之间的深层联系尚不清楚，因此需要建立更普适的理论框架。

Method: 通过推导欠阻达朗之万动力学下的精确有限时间涨落-响应等式，结合变分方法分析动态方差，并选择沿不可逆概率流方向的扰动来构建新的热力学不确定性关系。

Result: 得到了精确的有限时间涨落-响应等式，导出了紧致的响应界限和动态方差的变分表征；提出了一种可饱和的摩擦响应型热力学不确定性关系，并证明其对总熵产生的变分原理；发现传统速度分辨电流的不确定性因子可在驱动自由扩散中随耗散指数衰减，而新提出的摩擦响应因子保持普适下界。

Conclusion: 响应量而非平均电流本身，是连接欠阻尼动力学中涨落与耗散的核心物理量，该工作建立了更深刻且普适的热力学不确定性理论基础。

Abstract: Thermodynamic uncertainty relations connect current fluctuations to dissipation and are often rooted in fluctuation-response principles. In underdamped dynamics, however, conventional mean-current uncertainty relations can fail, while the underlying connection between fluctuations, response, and dissipation remains elusive. Here we uncover this structure by deriving an exact finite-time fluctuation-response equality for underdamped Langevin dynamics, valid for arbitrary time-dependent driving and general additive observables. The equality yields sharp response bounds and a variational characterization of the dynamically generated variance. Choosing the perturbation along the irreversible probability flow gives a friction-response thermodynamic uncertainty relation that can be saturated at any finite observation time, leading to an exact variational principle for the total entropy production. We further show that the conventional uncertainty factor of velocity-resolved currents can decay exponentially with dissipation even in driven free diffusion, while the friction-response factor retains its universal lower bound. These results establish response, rather than the mean current itself, as the quantity directly linking fluctuations and dissipation in underdamped dynamics.

</details>


### [200] [Learning Potts Models and $Z_3$ Toric Codes: Higher and Ordinary Nishimori Criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20268)
*Rushikesh A. Patil,Malte Pütz,Rohit Mukherjee,Guo-Yi Zhu,Simon Trebst,Andreas W. W. Ludwig*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了二维q态Potts模型在键能测量下的学习相图，发现了一条更高的Nishimori线及其临界点，揭示了丰富的相结构和RG流特性，并通过解析与数值方法验证了结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受先前Ising模型研究启发，探索q态Potts模型在测量影响下的相变行为，特别是更高Nishimori线的存在及其物理意义。

Method: 采用解析方法（基于高斯测量协议）和大规模数值计算，分析相图结构、固定点、RG流及Casimir有效中心荷的变化；结合Elitzur定理论证普通Nishimori临界点的稳定性。

Result: 发现了更高的Nishimori线与三临界点，确定其分隔顺磁、铁磁与类自旋玻璃相；获得多个普适量的精确结果；验证了c-effective定理预测的有效中心荷沿测量诱导RG流单调递减；明确了高Nishimori点作为信息临界点的意义。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了测量强度调控下Potts模型的学习相图理论框架，揭示了新型临界现象与相变结构，等价描述了一个被监测的变形Zq拓扑编码中的信息相变。

Abstract: Motivated by a previous Ising study, we identify a ${\it higher}$ Nishimori line in the learning phase diagram of the $2D$ $q$-state Potts model $(2 < q\leq 4)$ under bond-energy measurements. This ${\it higher}$ Nishimori line meets the critical temperature line of the Potts model, in a ${\it higher}$ Nishimori critical point -- a tricritical point at finite inference strength that separates a paramagnetic, a ferromagnetic and a 'spin-glass' phase. With analytical tools, we discuss the general structure of the rich phase diagram, which contains two unstable and three stable fixed points, and obtain a number of exact results for universal quantities, including the decay exponent of the Edwards-Anderson correlator, using a Gaussian measurement protocol which allows for exact calculations. Using extensive numerical tools, we confirm these statements for a generic, discrete $q$-state measurement protocol and determine precise numerical estimates for the location of higher and ordinary Nishimori critical points as well as RG flows between the various fixed points. We also discuss the Casimir effective central charges of the critical points in the learning phase diagram, and their monotonic ${\it decrease}$ along measurement-induced RG flows, as established non-perturbatively by the c-effective theorem and its extensions, and contrast it to the monotonic increase along the corresponding RG flows in the random-bond Potts model. Finally, we discuss a general argument based on ${\it Elitzur's \; theorem}$ that establishes stability of the ordinary Nishimori critical points in their respective learning phase diagrams. Equivalently, our results describe a monitored deformed $\mathbb{Z}_q$ toric code where the tricritical ${\it higher}$ Nishimori point is an 'information' critical point that separates stable quantum, classical, and no memory phases.

</details>


### [201] [Fluctuation-Controlled Asymmetric Kinetics in Metal-Insulator Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20100)
*Tapas Bar,David Pesquera,Timm Swoboda,Arnau Villalobos-Martin,Cristian Rodriguez-Tinoco,Pol Lloveras,Marianna Sledzinska,Javier Rodriguez-Viejo*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了1T-TaS$_2$中热驱动金属-绝缘体相变的动力学不对称性，发现冷却过程中相变以爆发式雪崩形式进行，而加热时则连续平滑，表明两者的非热性程度不同，从而决定了相变路径的选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解一级相变中滞后现象的起源及其动力学路径选择机制，特别是热扰动下相变路径的非对称行为。

Method: 结合输运、量热和拉曼测量，并利用脉冲焦耳加热产生受控的非平衡热扰动，研究1T-TaS$_2$中的相变动力学。

Result: 发现冷却过程中的相变具有爆发式雪崩特征且强非热性，而加热过程连续且由涨落逐步克服成核势垒；两种路径表现出不同的非热性程度。

Conclusion: 相变路径的选择由非热性程度控制，该结果为一级相变中的动力学路径调控提供了普适框架。

Abstract: We report asymmetric kinetics in thermally driven metal-insulator transitions (MITs) in 1T-TaS$_2$. Using combined transport, calorimetric, and Raman measurements, we show that the transition proceeds via burst-like avalanches during cooling, while remaining continuous during heating. Although bulk transport is masked by percolative conduction, local probes and thermal measurements reveal intrinsic asymmetry in the transformation pathways. Using controlled nonequilibrium thermal perturbations generated by pulsed Joule heating, we demonstrate that the phase-ordering dynamics remains strongly athermal during cooling, whereas during heating fluctuations progressively overcome nucleation barriers, leading to a smooth transformation. The distinct responses to thermal perturbations indicate different degrees of athermality of the two hysteresis branches, which govern the transformation pathways and give rise to the observed kinetic asymmetry. These results establish a general framework in which the degree of athermality controls pathway selection in first-order phase transitions.

</details>


### [202] [Biorthogonal Conformal Dynamics in Non-Hermitian Quantum Quenches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20156)
*Yifan Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于边界共形场论（BCFT）的框架，用于描述相互作用非厄米临界系统中的全局猝灭动力学，通过复数时间边界的解析延拓揭示了左右初态独立制备下的普适动力学行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非厄米临界系统中全局猝灭后的动力学演化，特别是在左右希尔伯特空间初态独立的情况下，传统量子力学框架不再适用，需发展新的理论工具。

Method: 利用边界共形场论方法，将欧几里得带状相关函数解析延拓到实时间，并引入左右独立的初态准备，结合线性与反线性对称配对来约束复数外推参数的几何结构。

Result: 在相互作用的Yang-Lee自旋链中验证了该理论，发现线性配对外推参数的虚部可预测反线性配一对点函数的时间中心（相对精度达10^{-3}），并控制局部相位演化和边界块的解析路径；进一步通过外场猝灭、混合左右初态及复五态Potts固定点等案例验证了框架的普适性。

Conclusion: 建立了适用于相互作用非厄米临界系统的BCFT框架，表明复数时间边界数据能够组织起猝灭后普适的动力学行为，为非厄米量子动力学提供了新的理论基础。

Abstract: Global quenches at one-dimensional critical points admit a boundary conformal field theory (BCFT) description in which Euclidean strip correlators are analytically continued to real time. We formulate this construction for interacting non-Hermitian critical systems, where a right ket alone does not specify the dynamical readout and the left covector is part of the microscopic quench protocol. Independent left and right preparations flowing to the same conformal boundary define the two temporal boundaries of a strip with generally complex one-sided extrapolation parameters; linear and antilinear symmetry pairings are exploited to constrain this geometry. In the interacting Yang-Lee spin chain, statically calibrated boundary data determine the biorthogonal dynamics of the complete-character return amplitude, a primary one-point function, and a spatial correlator. The imaginary part of the linear-paired extrapolation parameter predicts the temporal center of an independently evolved antilinear-paired one-point function at the $10^{-3}$ relative level; the same preparation phase controls local phase evolution and the analytic-continuation path of boundary blocks. Further results test this formalism with a direct field-on quench, mixed left and right preparations, and a complex five-state Potts fixed point with complex primary dimensions. These results establish a BCFT framework for biorthogonal global quenches in interacting non-Hermitian critical systems, in which complex temporal-boundary data organize universal post-quench dynamics.

</details>


### [203] [The Memory Hidden in Response Fluctuations: Trajectory-Level Fluctuation-Response Theory and Inequalities for Non-Markovian Jump Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20328)
*Jiming Zheng,Zhiyue Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对非马尔可夫跳跃过程的涨落-响应理论，利用事件记录中的历史依赖性，构建了基于鞅增量的正交基来描述可观测量的随机偏差，并导出了精确的有限时间和有限频率涨落-响应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代实验中常观测到依赖于历史的非平衡动力学过程，传统马尔可夫方法难以处理记忆效应，因此需要发展适用于非马尔可夫跳跃过程的涨落-响应理论。

Method: 通过分析事件记录的历史依赖均值趋势，将剩余的不可预测部分视为鞅增量，利用不同时间与转移之间的鞅增量正交性，构建可观测量波动的展开形式，并引入事件后果核作为展开系数。

Result: 建立了精确的有限时间与有限频率涨落-响应关系；发现响应异质性间隙是非负的，可用于检验记忆坐标的充分性；并推导出由动力活性控制的响应-动力学不确定性关系。

Conclusion: 该理论为非马尔可夫跳跃过程提供了严格的涨落-响应框架，揭示了历史依赖性在响应行为中的核心作用，且可通过自发轨迹进行估计，具有实验适用性。

Abstract: Modern experiments often record nonequilibrium dynamics as sequences of discrete events whose rates depend on the realized past. We develop a fluctuation-response theory for such non-Markovian jump processes directly on the observed event record. Memory can destroy a closed master equation for the state probabilities. Each transition count nevertheless obeys an exact stochastic equation. After the history-dependent mean event tendency is subtracted, the remaining random increment is a martingale increment--the part of the event that cannot be predicted from the past. Martingale increments associated with different transitions and different times are orthogonal. These increments form a complete orthogonal basis for the random deviation of any observable measured from the record, such as a current, occupation time, or event count. The coefficient of a given increment is the event-consequence kernel. It measures how that event changes the predicted final observable, relative to continuing without the event, for the particular history already realized. Multiplying this kernel by the event intensity gives the history-conditioned response to perturbing the corresponding transition rate. Thus, the intensity-normalized response is exactly the expansion coefficient of that event in the observable fluctuation. This identification yields exact finite-time and finite-frequency fluctuation-response relations. Averaging over histories leaves a nonnegative response-heterogeneity gap. The gap measures how strongly the consequence of the same event varies across histories and tests whether a proposed memory coordinate is response-sufficient. Finite-history versions can be estimated from spontaneous trajectories. Bounding the logarithmic rate sensitivity further gives response-kinetic uncertainty relations controlled by dynamical activity.

</details>
