# physics.soc-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [204] [Rural Commute Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19291)
*Mohamed Khalafalla,Doreen Kobelo,Thobias Sando,Janeroza Matyeni*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 该研究利用2017年NHTS调查数据，通过多项逻辑回归分析农村地区的通勤模式，发现92.1%的农村出行依赖私人交通工具，且社会人口特征、家庭特征和出行特征显著影响交通方式选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨农村地区交通方式选择的影响因素，以改善农村居民对就业和基本服务的可达性。

Method: 采用2017年国家家庭旅行调查（NHTS）数据，运用多项逻辑回归模型分析社会人口、家庭及出行特征对交通方式选择的影响。

Result: 研究发现92.1%的农村出行依赖私人车辆；老年人、女性和受教育程度较高的个体使用公共交通的可能性更低；家庭收入高、拥有更多车辆的家庭使用公共交通的概率更低；出行距离越长，步行或骑行等主动出行方式的可能性越低。

Conclusion: 农村地区对私人交通工具高度依赖，交通可达性受限，研究结果可为制定公平、可及的农村交通规划提供依据。

Abstract: Transportation provides access to employment opportunities and essential services such as healthcare services, while urban areas have various transportation options, the situation differs in rural areas. Rural residents often have longer commute distances, limited access to public transit, and extended waiting times for public transportation if they exist, which can significantly impact their access to vital services and job opportunities. This study used data from the 2017 NHTS survey to examine the commuting patterns in rural areas by utilizing multinomial logistic regression to determine how various factors impact the choice of mode of transport in rural areas. Findings from this study revealed a higher dependency, 92.1%, on using personal vehicles when making trips in rural areas. Multinomial logistic regression results showed that socio-demographics, household, and trip characteristics affect the mode of transport used in a trip. Older adults, females, and individuals with higher education levels than high school graduates are less likely to use public transit when making trips. For household characteristics, the availability of vehicles in a household and households with higher income levels have lower probabilities of making a trip using public transit. Longer trip distances reduce the likelihood of a trip using active commuting modes such as walking and biking. These findings provide insights into understanding the transportation behaviors in rural areas and provide knowledge to be used in the planning and developing of transportation projects to promote equitable and accessible transportation in rural areas.

</details>


### [205] [Analysis of the convergence of the degree distribution of contracting random networks towards a Poisson distribution using the relative entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2009.04249)
*I. Tishby,O. Biham,E. Katzav*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在节点删除过程中随机网络的结构演化，证明了配置模型网络的度分布会收敛于泊松分布，进而其结构趋近于 Erdős-Rényi 图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解网络在收缩过程中的结构变化，特别是不同节点删除机制对网络拓扑的影响。

Method: 利用相对熵作为度分布与泊松分布之间的距离度量，推导收缩过程中相对熵的时间导数，并通过严格论证展示其单调递减至零。

Result: 在随机删除、优先删除和传播删除三种场景下，配置模型网络的度分布在收缩过程中逐渐趋近泊松分布，网络结构趋于无关联的 Erdős-Rényi 图。

Conclusion: 无论初始度分布如何（包括规则图、指数分布和幂律分布），收缩后的网络结构最终都趋向于具有相同平均度的泊松分布，验证了先前通过数值模拟得到的结果。

Abstract: We present analytical results for the structural evolution of random networks undergoing contraction processes via generic node deletion scenarios, namely, random deletion, preferential deletion and propagating deletion. Focusing on configuration model networks, which exhibit a given degree distribution $P_0(k)$ and no correlations, we show using a rigorous argument that upon contraction the degree distributions of these networks converge towards a Poisson distribution. To this end, we use the relative entropy $S_t=S[P_t(k) || π(k|\langle K \rangle_t)]$ of the degree distribution $P_t(k)$ of the contracting network at time $t$ with respect to the corresponding Poisson distribution $π(k|\langle K \rangle_t)$ with the same mean degree $\langle K \rangle_t$ as a distance measure between $P_t(k)$ and Poisson. The relative entropy is suitable as a distance measure since it satisfies $S_t \ge 0$ for any degree distribution $P_t(k)$, while equality is obtained only for $P_t(k) = π(k|\langle K \rangle_t)$. We derive an equation for the time derivative $dS_t/dt$ during network contraction and show that the relative entropy decreases monotonically to zero during the contraction process. We thus conclude that the degree distributions of contracting configuration model networks converge towards a Poisson distribution. Since the contracting networks remain uncorrelated, this means that their structures converge towards an Erd{\H o}s-Rényi (ER) graph structure, substantiating earlier results obtained using direct integration of the master equation and computer simulations [I. Tishby, O. Biham and E. Katzav, {\it Phys. Rev. E} {\bf 100}, 032314 (2019)]. We demonstrate the convergence for configuration model networks with degenerate degree distributions (random regular graphs), exponential degree distributions and power-law degree distributions (scale-free networks).

</details>


### [206] [Second-Order Continuum Model for Disordered Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19440)
*Shashank Rajput,Venkatesan Kanagaraj,Martin Treiber,Gowri Asaithambi,Ostap Okhrin*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种二维二阶宏观交通流模型，用于捕捉无车道或车道纪律较弱的复杂交通动力学，通过引入纵向与横向耦合相互作用，并考虑道路边界效应，能够再现激波传播、侧向扩散等关键交通特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更好地描述无车道或车道纪律较弱交通系统中的复杂动态，传统一维模型难以刻画车辆的横向移动和空间共享行为，因此需要扩展至二维并显式考虑纵横向耦合及道路边界影响。

Method: 基于经典连续性方程扩展至二维空间，结合FVDM构建纵向加速度，采用OVM一致的交互原则建模横向动力学，并引入道路边界相关项；采用迎风法和Lax-Friedrichs格式进行数值求解。

Result: 数值实验表明该模型能有效再现激波传播、侧向扩散、车辆重排和跨道路宽度的稳定密度演化等现象，在自由流、中度拥堵和严重拥堵条件下均表现良好。

Conclusion: 所提出的二维宏观交通流模型为无车道或弱车道系统的交通动力学提供了鲁棒且一致的建模框架，具有良好的理论意义与应用前景。

Abstract: In this study, a two-dimensional second-order macroscopic traffic flow model is proposed to capture the complex dynamics of disordered traffic by explicitly incorporating coupled longitudinal and lateral interactions. The framework extends the classical continuity equation to two spatial dimensions and introduces acceleration equations consisting of self-driven, interaction-induced, and road-boundary-related components. The macroscopic longitudinal acceleration is formulated based on the Full Velocity Difference Model (FVDM), while lateral dynamics are governed by interaction principles consistent with the Optimal Velocity Model (OVM). The explicit inclusion of road-boundary effects enables the representation of vehicle confinement and space-sharing behavior that are essential in lane-free and weakly lane-disciplined traffic systems. The model is examined through a series of numerical experiments in which longitudinal and lateral dynamics are analysed individually as well as simultaneously within a coupled two-dimensional setting under a range of initial conditions, including transitions between free-flow, medium congestion, and heavy congestion, and different lateral density configurations. The simulations demonstrate the model's ability to reproduce key traffic features such as shockwave propagation, lateral dispersion, vehicle rearrangement, and stable density evolution across the road width. The numerical scheme, based on upwind and Lax-Friedrichs discretisations, ensures stable solutions of the coupled partial differential equations. Overall, the proposed framework provides a robust macroscopic description of disordered traffic and offers a consistent basis for analysing two-dimensional vehicular flow dynamics.

</details>


### [207] [Emergence of cooperation: A reputation-modulated reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20016)
*Chenyang Zhao,Jiqiang Zhang,Li Chen,Yong Zou*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于强化学习的空间囚徒困境模型，通过引入局部声誉机制来研究声誉如何作为信息影响个体决策，促进合作行为的涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有博弈论模型多将声誉视为调节收益或策略更新的外部因素，而忽视了声誉作为信息在社会互动中对个体认知的影响。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，探讨声誉如何通过改变个体的信息处理方式来促进合作。

Method: 构建了一个基于Q学习的空间囚徒困境模型，代理结合个体经验与社会信息，并利用局部定义的声誉指标进行决策。

Result: 发现声誉调节的学习显著促进了合作行为的出现，并观察到随着诱惑增加，系统从完全合作到完全背叛存在不连续相变。合作通过合作集群的成核扩散，而这些集群的瓦解导致系统进入完全背叛的吸收态。

Conclusion: 声誉不仅通过直接激励促进合作，还通过重塑个体依赖的社会信息环境，在学习与适应过程中发挥关键作用。

Abstract: Reputation is widely recognized as a key mechanism for sustaining cooperation. However, most existing game-theoretic models treat reputation primarily as an external factor that modulates payoffs, interaction structures, or strategy update rules. In many social contexts, though, reputation operates primarily as information -- it shapes how individuals interpret their own experiences and assess the behavior of others. To bridge this gap, we propose a spatial prisoner's dilemma game grounded in the reinforcement learning paradigm, in which agents equipped with Q-learning integrate both individual and social information via a locally defined reputation metric to guide their decisions. Our results reveal that reputation-modulated learning significantly promotes the emergence of cooperative behavior, and we observe a discontinuous phase transition from full cooperation to full defection as the temptation increases. Cooperation spreads through the nucleation of cooperative clusters, whereas the disintegration of these clusters drives the system into an absorbing state of complete defection. Overall, this study demonstrates that reputation facilitates cooperation not only by providing direct incentives but also by reshaping the social information landscape that agents rely on for learning and adaptation.

</details>


### [208] [Shift or curtail? How much data-center flexibility is worth depends on the host power grid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19622)
*Saroj Khanal,Geon Roh,Boyu Yao,Abraham Silverman,Dennice Gayme,Charalambos Konstantinou,Jip Kim,Yury Dvorkin*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了数据中心灵活运营对电力系统成本和基础设施投资的影响，发现灵活性的价值取决于电网特性和政策目标，在PJM地区以空间转移为主，在韩国则以时间转移为主。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心的快速增长可能导致电网基础设施过度建设并造成资本搁浅，因此需要探索灵活运营模式以延缓基础设施投资。

Method: 将数据中心负荷分为固定、灵活和可中断三类，并将其嵌入到容量扩展模型中，应用于市场主导的PJM区域和碳排放受限的韩国电网进行分析。

Result: 在PJM，通过区域间工作负载转移，2028年和2038年系统成本分别降低6%和19%，避免了4.4吉瓦和8.9吉瓦的燃气和核能发电装机；在韩国，将负载转移到中午太阳能高峰时段，使得2028年可新增0.5吉瓦太阳能，2038年避免1.2吉瓦燃气和0.3吉瓦电池投资；事件形状限制会削弱削减负荷的价值。

Conclusion: 灵活性采购及其价值受电网特性与政策目标驱动，合理的灵活性机制可显著降低系统成本并优化能源结构。

Abstract: Data-center growth risks overbuilding power grid infrastructure and stranding capital. Flexible data-center operation can defer infrastructure investments, but its value depends on the flexibility mechanism and the host power grid characteristics. We classify data-center load as firm, flexible or interruptible, and embed them in capacity expansion applied to market-organized, fossil-heavy PJM and carbon-capped, centrally coordinated Korea. In PJM, the flexibility value is spatial: shifting workloads between zones reduces system cost by 6% in 2028 and 19% in 2038, avoiding 4.4 GW and 8.9 GW of gas and nuclear generation. In Korea, it is temporal: shifting load into midday solar hours makes 0.5 GW of additional solar worth building in 2028 and avoids 1.2 GW of gas and 0.3 GW of batteries in 2038. In both, realistic event-shape limits diminish the value of curtailment. The results show that flexibility procurement and its value are driven by grid characteristics and policy objectives.

</details>


### [209] [The effect of preferential node deletion on the structure of networks that evolve via preferential attachment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.16574)
*Barak Budnick,Ofer Biham,Eytan Katzav*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在节点增长和优先连接机制下，优先删除节点对网络结构的影响，提出了PAPD模型，并发现当增长/收缩率η处于特定范围时，度分布会收敛到具有指数尾的稳态分布，而在纯增长情况下（η=1）则呈现幂律尾，揭示了在η=1处的相变现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在网络演化过程中，优先删除策略如何影响网络的度分布特性，特别是与随机删除相比，优先删除引起的结构性变化。

Method: 采用主方程方法和生成函数形式化手段，分析PAPD模型中时间依赖的度分布Pt(k)，并推导其稳态分布特性。

Result: 发现对于每个m>0存在临界值ηc(m)，当η超过该值时度分布收敛至稳态；在η=1时为幂律尾（无标度网络特征），而η<1时为指数尾，表现出有明确尺度的分布，且在η=1处出现相变。

Conclusion: 优先删除显著改变网络的度分布性质，在η=1处发生从无标度到有标度网络的相变，不同于PARD模型中只要η>0就保持幂律尾的结果。

Abstract: We present analytical results for the effect of preferential node deletion on the structure of networks that evolve via node addition and preferential attachment. To this end, we consider a preferential-attachment-preferential-deletion (PAPD) model, in which at each time step, with probability $P_{\rm add}$ there is a growth step where an isolated node is added to the network, followed by the addition of $m$ edges, where each edge connects a node selected uniformly at random to a node selected preferentially in proportion to its degree. Alternatively, with probability $P_{\rm del}=1-P_{\rm add}$ there is a contraction step, in which a preferentially selected node is deleted and its links are erased. The balance between the growth and contraction processes is captured by the growth/contraction rate $η=P_{\rm add}-P_{\rm del}$. For $0 < η\le 1$ the overall process is of network growth, while for $-1\leη<0$ the overall process is of network contraction. Using the master equation and the generating function formalism, we study the time-dependent degree distribution $P_t(k)$. It is found that for each value of $m>0$ there is a critical value $η_c(m)=-(m-2)/(m+2)$ such that for $η_c(m)<η\le1$ the degree distribution $P_t(k)$ converges towards a stationary distribution $P_{\rm st}(k)$. In the special case of pure growth, where $η=1$, the model is reduced to a preferential attachment growth model and $P_{\rm st}(k)$ exhibits a power-law tail, which is a characteristic of scale-free networks. In contrast, for $η_c(m)<η<1$ the distribution $P_{\rm st}(k)$ exhibits an exponential tail, which has a well-defined scale.This implies a phase transition at $η=1$, in contrast with the preferential-attachment-random-deletion (PARD) model [B. Budnick, O. Biham and E. Katzav, J. Stat. Mech. 013401 (2025)], in which the power-law tail remains intact as long as $η>0$.

</details>


### [210] [Climate change and human mobility will shape dengue emergence risk in Europe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20079)
*Charley Presigny,Paolo Baglioni,Pietro Rotondo,Michele Allegra,Annalisa Barla,Manlio De Domenico*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本研究通过整合气候、人口和流动性数据，构建了一个多尺度模型来预测21世纪欧洲登革热的传播风险，发现气候变化将显著增加风险，并强调需将人口流动和气候驱动的人口再分布纳入预测模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于气候变化导致欧洲蚊媒病毒暴发风险上升，而该地区历史上为非流行区，因此亟需识别潜在暴露人群及其条件，以增强疫情应对能力。

Method: 开发了一个集成的多尺度模型，结合机制性传播引擎、媒介丰度框架和基于移动性的元群体系统，并融合气候、人口和航空交通数据，同时引入气候驱动的人口迁移模型来评估登革热风险。

Result: 在多数排放情景下，模型显示登革热风险指标显著上升；尽管传播仍主要依赖输入病例，但在最严重排放情景下正逐步转向环境驱动模式。

Conclusion: 为更好预测和管理温带地区蚊媒疾病暴发，必须将人类流动性路径和气候驱动的人口再分布纳入疾病预测模型。

Abstract: The risk of local arbovirus outbreaks in Europe is expected to increase due to climate change, as suggested by the multiplication of arbovirus outbreaks in the last decades. Europe has historically been a non-endemic region, making it vital to pinpoint which populations are potentially exposed -and under which conditions- so we can build truly robust epidemic preparedness capabilities. We introduce an integrated, multi-scale model that fuses a mechanistic transmission engine with a vector abundance framework, all embedded in a mobility-driven metapopulation system capturing human, vector, and air-traffic movement. To this end, we combine climate and population projections with mobility data to estimate and map dengue emergence risk in Europe throughout the 21st century. Additionally, we introduce a dedicated migration model that explores how climate-driven population redistribution could alter these risk estimates.Assuming the climate avoids major tipping points, model-derived risk indicators increase substantially under most emissions scenarios. While the spatio-temporal risk will remain largely driven by importation, our results indicate a gradual transition toward an environment-driven regime, particularly under the worst-case emissions scenario. To better anticipate and manage recurrent arbovirus outbreaks, our findings highlight the need to integrate mobility pathways and climate-driven population redistribution into predictive models of vector-borne disease emergence in temperate regions.

</details>


### [211] [Growth Without Us: Machine Consumers, Corporate Circularity, and the Decoupling of GDP from Humanity after AGI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20231)
*Sahil Sharma*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在通用人工智能（AGI）出现后的后稀缺经济中，人类不再参与生产与消费时的宏观经济结构，提出经济增长可脱离人类需求而通过全自动化企业自我 reinvestment 实现最大化增长，并强调人类福利完全取决于其对企业网络的所有权份额，政策重点应从就业转向所有权分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为若人类无收入则无法购买全自动经济的产出，从而质疑全自动化可行性；本文旨在澄清这一误解，建立一个无需人类参与的需求闭环经济模型，并分析其增长潜力与对人类福利的影响机制。

Method: 构建一个由拥有AI与机器人代理的企业组成的封闭经济模型，其中所有产出均用于再投资；采用类冯·诺依曼扩展经济框架，分析增长率、瓶颈转移及人类所有权份额动态，推导出黄金律解耦定理，并刻画不同终端制度下的政策工具。

Result: （i）实现了需求闭合，形成零人类消费但正增长的非退化经济；（ii）增长瓶颈从人口限制转向制造与能源，增速可提高1-2个数量级；（iii）人类福利与GDP完全解耦，仅依赖于其企业所有权份额ε_t；当r=g时，任何人类消费都将导致ε_t指数衰减；提出了三种终端状态及其调控机制。

Conclusion: 在后AGI经济中，就业政策失效，所有权分配政策成为决定人类长期福祉的核心。

Abstract: The standard objection to full automation is demand-side: if humans earn nothing, who buys the output? This confuses an accounting role with a biological species. We model a post-AGI economy in which corporations own populations of AI and robotic agents that are both producers and consumers of energy, compute, maintenance, and upgrades, traded among firms. Three results follow. (i) Demand closure: a closed inter-corporate economy with zero human consumption is not degenerate; it is the classical von Neumann expanding economy, whose growth rate is well defined, positive, and maximal precisely because all output is reinvested. (ii) Bottleneck removal: once economic agents are manufactured rather than reared, the binding constraint on growth shifts from human demography (a ~20-year, non-parallelizable reproduction technology capped at a few percent per year) to fabrication throughput and energy capture, permitting growth one to two orders of magnitude higher, with hyperbolic episodes when machine researchers raise their own productivity. (iii) Decoupling: output and human welfare separate completely, and the welfare relevance of arbitrarily large GDP collapses into one state variable: the human ownership share $ε_t$ of the corporate network. A golden-rule decoupling theorem sharpens this. At maximal growth the interest rate equals the growth rate (r = g), so any positive human consumption rate out of wealth makes $ε_t$ decay exponentially at exactly that rate. The human share survives only if the machine economy runs strictly inside its expansion frontier, or if law forces it to. We characterize three terminal regimes -- rentier post-scarcity, full circular decoupling, socialized ownership -- and the instruments that select among them. The conclusion is narrow: in a post-AGI economy, employment policy is obsolete and ownership policy is everything.

</details>
